Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The machine_learning variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 44 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 787 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the machine_learning space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 44 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 787. QED.
